# Changelog

All notable changes to the Genesis Mission documentation repository will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [3.24.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (ChipNexus — §3.1 & A.3, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded **ChipNexus** (formerly **Primis AI**) from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.1 Industry, Hyperscale & Hardware Commitments → B. Quantum Developers & Semiconductor Foundries`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the *grants and commitments* layer (founded **2023** as Primis AI by University of Utah ECE professor **Pierre-Emmanuel Gaillardon** as Founder and President with **Valerio Tenace** as Co-Founder and CTO, the **July 2026** rebrand to ChipNexus alongside the **NEX** platform launch and the close of a seed round led by **Cambium Capital**, and the company's role as **industry collaborator** — not prime awardee — on the University of Washington-led Phase I Genesis project *Neuromorphic Terahertz Imaging via Analog Compute-in-Memory in AI-Driven Augmented Reality Hardware* led by **Hossein Naghavi** of the **TIME Lab** with co-investigator **Morteza Fayazi** (University of Utah ECE), **Texas A&M University** and **NVIDIA**, one of **278 awards from more than 5,000 national applications**); the *technical capabilities* substrate (the **NEX** multi-agent, self-correcting agentic AI flow spanning **RTL generation, review and refactoring**, digital and analog **simulation** setup, iterative **verification failure triage**, **logic synthesis** coordination and **floor planning, layout and timing/power** analysis through a command-line interface, integrating with existing commercial and open-source **EDA toolchains**, source repositories, automation scripts and CI/CD rather than replacing them, deployable on public cloud, private cloud and **on-premises** with out-of-the-box **Google Gemini** support and an enterprise **bring-your-own-model** option, succeeding the Primis AI **RapidGPT** HDL assistant with **AutoCheck**, **AutoFix** and **AutoDoc**, and a vendor-stated **43×** productivity figure whose underlying study is unpublished); and a *Mission Domains* sub-bullet placing ChipNexus as the **design-automation layer** of a microelectronics co-design loop fusing **analog compute-in-memory** inference into a **terahertz imaging** front end for a low-power augmented-reality headset (firefighting and emergency response, autonomous navigation, security screening, industrial inspection, biomedical sensing and beyond-5G communication), shortening the mixed-signal iteration cycle for bespoke neuromorphic imagers against the project's **real-time digital twin**, alongside **Synopsys**, **NVIDIA** and the Michigan **ArchEvolve** agentic co-design effort.

### Changed
- **Coverage Tracker (`coverage.md`)**: Upgraded **ChipNexus** from `❌ Not Covered` to `✅ Full Profile`, set its `Process Count` to **1**, its `Paper Section` to `§3.1, A.3`, corrected its link to [chipnexus.ai](https://chipnexus.ai/), and replaced its notes with the Primis AI founding and rebrand, the NEX agentic flow and deployment options, the RapidGPT lineage and the UW-led terahertz imaging collaboration. Recalculated the summary metrics: Industry Partners **90 → 91** full / **3 → 2** not covered, totals **205 → 206** full profiles and **33 → 32** not covered, coverage level **86.1% → 86.6%** (**13.9% → 13.4%** not covered), with the header scope line and the closing note updated accordingly.
- **Appendix A.3 (`README.md` & `README.de.md`)**: Added a **ChipNexus** row to the *Semiconductor, EDA & Industrial Partners* tables in both editions.
- **References (`references.md`)**: Retained all existing entries and appended the *Primis AI Becomes ChipNexus and Launches NEX for Agentic Chip-Design Automation* announcement and the University of Utah ECE *Morteza Fayazi Among First Class of Genesis Mission Awardees* release (also listed under the University of Utah in `## 5. National Labs & University Coverage`), plus a ChipNexus entry under `## 2. Collaborators`.
- **Version Bump**: Incremented the version string to `3.24.0` across `README.md` and `README.de.md`.

## [3.23.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (Deployable Energy — §3.1 & A.3, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded **Deployable Energy** from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.1 Industry, Hyperscale & Hardware Commitments`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the *grants and commitments* layer (Houston-based developer co-founded and led by CEO **Bobby Gallagher**, selection for the DOE **Nuclear Energy Launch Pad** run by the **National Reactor Innovation Center (NRIC)** at Idaho National Laboratory as an extension of the **Reactor Pilot Program** created under **Executive Order 14301**, a **DOE-approved Preliminary Documented Safety Analysis cleared in 106 days**, criticality roughly **150 days** after project kickoff, the privately borne cost model with DOE contributing site access, fuel logistics and laboratory expertise, a **$19 Million Series A** led by **Atreides Management** with Angle Technologies, Northpond Ventures and Second Century Ventures, and the August 2026 selection for an **INL full-power demonstration** plus the **Hornbeck Offshore** MOU for a shipboard **maritime demonstration** supported by **Lloyd's Register**); the *technical capabilities* substrate (**Unity**, the *Unity Nuclear Battery*, a **~1 MWe-class water-moderated, gas-cooled microreactor** running on standard **low-enriched uranium dioxide (~5%)** rather than HALEU, with an actively cooled **helium primary loop**, a factory-manufactured core in a **standard 20-foot shipping container** and a target of more than **five years** of full-power operation between refuelings); and a *Mission Domains* sub-bullet recording the **June 30, 2026** initial criticality at **Idaho National Laboratory** — the **third advanced reactor criticality** delivered ahead of the **July 4, 2026** goal alongside **Antares Nuclear**, **Valar Atomics** and the **Radiant** DOME experiment — as a source of fast-cadence design-to-criticality, safety-analysis, reactivity, controls and integration datasets for the INL-led nuclear AI campaign (plant design and licensing, semi-autonomous operations, manufacturing, fuel-cycle qualification), with the planned maritime demonstration adding a mobile, non-grid-tied operating envelope.

### Changed
- **Coverage Tracker (`coverage.md`)**: Upgraded **Deployable Energy** from `❌ Not Covered` to `✅ Full Profile`, set its `Process Count` to **1**, its `Paper Section` to `§3.1, A.3`, and replaced its notes with the Unity design, the Nuclear Energy Launch Pad criticality, the safety-analysis and schedule records, the Series A financing and the INL full-power and Hornbeck maritime demonstrations. Recalculated the summary metrics: Industry Partners **89 → 90** full / **4 → 3** not covered, totals **204 → 205** full profiles and **34 → 33** not covered, coverage level **85.7% → 86.1%** (**14.3% → 13.9%** not covered), with the header scope line and the closing note updated accordingly.
- **Appendix A.3 (`README.md` & `README.de.md`)**: Added a **Deployable Energy** row to the *Energy, Utilities & Critical Materials* partner tables in both editions.
- **References (`references.md`)**: Retained all existing entries and appended the DOE *U.S. Department of Energy Meets President Trump's Goal, Delivers Third Advanced Reactor Criticality* announcement, the Deployable Energy *Unity Demonstration Reactor Achieves Criticality at Idaho National Laboratory* release, the *Selected for Full-Power Demonstration at Idaho National Laboratory and Maritime Demonstration with Hornbeck Offshore* release and the NRIC **Nuclear Energy Launch Pad** portal, plus a Deployable Energy entry under `## 2. Collaborators`.

## [3.22.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (Antares Nuclear — §3.1 & A.3, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded **Antares Nuclear (Antares Industries, Inc.)** from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.1 Industry, Hyperscale & Hardware Commitments`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the *grants and commitments* layer (founded **2023** by CEO **Jordan Bramble** in Torrance, California; selection for the DOE **Reactor Pilot Program** created under **Executive Order 14301** to bring at least three privately funded reactors to criticality by **July 4, 2026**; DOE-approved **Preliminary Documented Safety Analysis** and **HALEU** allocation from Idaho National Laboratory's downblended research-reactor inventory; over **$600 Million** raised including a **$96 Million Series B** (December 2025) and a **$470 Million Series C** (**July 27, 2026**, $370 Million equity plus $100 Million debt co-led by **Paradigm** and **Caffeinated Capital**); and programme relationships with the **U.S. Air Force**, **U.S. Space Force**, **U.S. Army**, the **Defense Innovation Unit**, **NASA** and **BWX Technologies** for TRISO fuel fabrication); the *technical capabilities* substrate (**Mark-0** sodium **heat-pipe-cooled** microreactor with **HALEU TRISO** compacts in a graphite-moderated core, a solid-state primary heat path with no pumps, coolant loops or water requirement and passive decay-heat removal, built as a zero-power physics validation article, plus the **Mark-1** production unit adding **closed-cycle Brayton** conversion for ~**100 kWe – 1 MWe** with electricity generation targeted for **2027** and fielded deployments from **2028**); and a *Mission Domains* sub-bullet recording the **June 4, 2026** zero-power criticality at **Idaho National Laboratory** — the **first advanced reactor critical under the Reactor Pilot Program**, the first privately developed non-light-water reactor to go critical in the United States in more than four decades and the first novel reactor at INL in over 50 years — as a source of first-of-a-kind criticality, reactivity-feedback and instrumentation datasets for the INL-led nuclear AI campaign (plant design and licensing, semi-autonomous operations, manufacturing, fuel-cycle qualification) and for the Mission's interest in firm, transportable, water-independent generation for defense installations, remote instrumentation and edge AI compute.

### Changed
- **Coverage Tracker (`coverage.md`)**: Upgraded **Antares Nuclear** from `❌ Not Covered` to `✅ Full Profile`, set its `Process Count` to **1**, its `Paper Section` to `§3.1, A.3`, and replaced its notes with the Mark-0 heat-pipe design, the Reactor Pilot Program criticality, the Mark-1 production unit, the funding history and the defense/space programme relationships. ## [3.21.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (University of Chicago — §3.3 & A.5, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded the **University of Chicago** from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.3 University Research Partners → Quantum Science, Information Theory & Modality Development`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the *laboratory stewardship and management contracts* layer (**UChicago Argonne, LLC** as sole-member operator of **Argonne National Laboratory** under DOE prime contract **DE-AC02-06CH11357** since **1 October 2006**, extended on **14 July 2026** for the term **1 October 2026 – 30 September 2031**, ~**$17.3 Billion** cumulative obligations against a **>$1 Billion** annual laboratory operating budget, **Board of Governors** chaired by President **Paul Alivisatos** with **Tracey Quinn** as secretary; plus lead partnership in **Fermi Forward Discovery Group, LLC** with **URA**, **Amentum Environment & Energy** and **Longenecker & Associates**, holding the **Fermilab** M&O contract from **1 January 2025** to **31 December 2029** and extendable to **fifteen years**, succeeding **Fermi Research Alliance, LLC** (2007–2024) — making UChicago the only university contractually accountable for two Genesis Mission host laboratories); the *quantum ecosystem and regional co-design* substrate (**Chicago Quantum Exchange** founded **2017** at the **Pritzker School of Molecular Engineering** under **David Awschalom**, anchoring Argonne, Fermilab, UIUC, UW–Madison, Northwestern and Purdue with 20+ corporate partners; the **Duality** accelerator launched **April 2021** by the **Polsky Center** with UIUC, Argonne and **P33**; the **IBM Quantum System Two** and **National Quantum Algorithm Center** at the **128-acre IQMP** with **PsiQuantum** as anchor tenant; PME modality faculty **Awschalom**, **Liang Jiang**, **Andrew Cleland**, **Peter Maurer**, **Frederic Chong** and **William Fefferman**; and UChicago's place among the **11 university partners** of the Argonne-led **Q-NEXT** center renewed **4 November 2025** for **$125 Million over five years** inside DOE's **$625 Million** five-center renewal); the *AI-for-science institutes and data infrastructure* layer (**Data Science Institute** under **Michael J. Franklin** and **Dan Nicolae**, the **AI+Science** initiative under Faculty Director of AI **Rebecca Willett**, the **Eric and Wendy Schmidt AI in Science** fellowship, and **Ian Foster**'s UChicago–Argonne dual appointment behind the **Globus** data-management fabric); and a *Mission Domains* sub-bullet placing **Aurora**, **Solstice** (100,000 NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs), **Equinox** (10,000 GPUs), the upgraded **Advanced Photon Source** and Argonne's Genesis data assets under a single university-governed contractual umbrella, framed by the laboratory's **80th anniversary** (chartered **1 July 1946**).

## [3.20.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission — §3.4 & A.1, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded the **U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC)** from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.4 Federal Agencies & Policy Bodies`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the statutory mandate and structure (**Energy Reorganization Act of 1974**, five-member bipartisan Commission under Chairman **Ho K. Nieh** with Commissioners **David A. Wright**, **Bradley Crowell**, **Matthew Marzano** and **Doug Weaver**, Rockville, Maryland headquarters, ~**2,600 FTE**, ~**$892 Million** FY 2027 request, licensing authority under **Title 10 CFR**); the executive-directed reform under **Executive Order 14300** (*Ordering the Reform of the Nuclear Regulatory Commission*, **May 23, 2025**) mandating a wholesale regulation and guidance revision (**9-month** NOPR, **18-month** final rules), fixed **18-month** new-reactor and **12-month** renewal decision deadlines, reconsideration of radiation-protection standards, **NEIMA** fixed fee caps and reorganization into three business lines; the final **10 CFR Part 53** risk-informed, technology-inclusive advanced reactor framework (March 2026, Federal Register **April 3, 2026**, first new licensing pathway since Part 52 in 1989) with **≤18-month** target reviews enabling the DOE **Reactor Pilot Program** designs (Radiant, Valar Atomics, Antares Nuclear, Deployable Energy) and **ADVANCE Act of 2024** high-volume licensing procedures; the **Artificial Intelligence Strategic Plan FY 2023–2027** (**NUREG-2261**) with its five regulatory-readiness goals; and a *Genesis Mission Coupling* sub-bullet recording the **ADAMS** public corpus (**3 million+** full-text documents) as the substrate for the Mission's nuclear language models and semantic search stack (**Atomic Canyon** at INL/ORNL, **Everstar Gordian AI**'s **208-page** one-day **NRIC** Generic **HTGR** license application) with the NRC retained as the human-accountable adjudicator under the **"design–AI–validate"** model rate-limiting the **400 GW by 2050** expansion.

## [3.19.0] - 2026-09-01

### Added
- **Entity Profile (Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility — §3.2 & A.5, README.md & README.de.md)**: Expanded the **Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility (OLCF / ORNL NCCS)** from a reference-only listing to a dedicated profile under `### 3.2 National Laboratories`, structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the facility charter as a **DOE Office of Science user facility** hosted in ORNL's **National Center for Computational Sciences**, founded **1992** as the Center for Computational Sciences, designated a leadership computing facility in **2004**, directed by **Arjun Shankar**, with the **Jaguar → Titan → Summit → Frontier** platform lineage; the **Frontier** exascale substrate (**9,408 HPE Cray EX235a** nodes, AMD Optimized 3rd Gen **EPYC "Trento"** plus **4 × Instinct MI250X** per node, **Slingshot-11** 200 Gbps dragonfly fabrics, **1.206 Exaflops Rmax** improved to **1.353 Exaflops** under 2025 tuning, ~**21–25 MW**, **4 TB** node-local NVMe) on the **Orion** Lustre file system (~**700 PB**, ~**5 TB/s** peak write, **10 PB** flash metadata tier) following the **November 15, 2024** decommissioning of **Summit**; the Genesis AI platforms **Lux** (HPE ProLiant Compute XD685, **MI355X**, EPYC, Pensando Ethernet, Slurm + Kubernetes under **DOE Moderate** controls, **3.5 million node-hours** split **50/50** between Genesis science and paid proprietary use) and **Discovery** (hardware **2028**, **HPE Cray Supercomputing GX5000**, 6th Gen **EPYC "Venice"**, **4 × MI430X** per node, **K3000 DAOS** storage at ~**300%** higher IOPS/rack, ~**10×** application productivity over Frontier); the **INCITE** (joint with ALCF, ~60% of allocatable time, **81 projects** in the 2025 cycle, 10% early-career reserve), **ALCC** and **Director's Discretionary** allocation programs; the **Quantum Computing User Program (QCUP)** brokering **IBM Quantum**, **Quantinuum**, **IonQ** and **IQM** access under the **QRUC**; and a *Mission Domains* sub-bullet recording the leadership capacity behind ORNL's Genesis flagship portfolio, its federation with **AmSC**/**HPDF** through NCCS provenance frameworks (**Flowcept**, **INTERSECT**) and the annual **OLCF User Summit**.

the **Rescale Intelligent Computing Platform** control plane placing workloads by policy across **AWS, Azure, Google Cloud, OCI and NVIDIA DGX Cloud** while federating on-premises **Slurm** clusters, executing on hundreds of pre-tuned **coretypes** (Intel/AMD CPUs, NVIDIA A100/H100/Blackwell-class GPUs, Arm, up to 400 Gbps interconnects, NVMe tiers) beneath **1,200+ pre-integrated solvers** with license queuing, version-pinned environments, RBAC, budget policies and PLM/SPDM APIs; the **AI Physics Operating System** automating surrogate dataset curation, training, validation and inference (up to **1,000×** speedup at >98% fidelity for steady-state CFD and static FEA, demonstrated with **GM Motorsports**) with Autodesk, Blender and **NVIDIA Omniverse** plugins and agents automating error detection, hardware selection, data structuring and reporting; and the governed federal posture of **FedRAMP Moderate**, active **ITAR** registration, **NIST SP 800-171/DFARS** CUI controls, **ISO 27001**, **SOC 2 Type II** and TISAX, the June 2024 **Defense Innovation Unit Success Memorandum** following the DoD **HPCMP** prototype and federation of DoD **DSRCs** with commercial cloud. ## [3.13.40] - 2026-09-01

### Changed
- **Entity Reprocessing (Arcee AI — §3.1 & A.3, README.md & README.de.md)**: Reprocessed the **Arcee AI** profile under `### 3.1 Industry, Hyperscale & Hardware Commitments`. The formerly single-paragraph entry is now structured into technical sub-bullets covering: the DOE collaboration announced in July 2026 as an open-science counterweight to closed frontier models, privately underwritten by Arcee AI (founded 2023, CEO Mark McQuade) rather than direct federal funding, with datasets and research environments solicited through the **Argonne National Laboratory contribution portal** (`genesisopenmodels.anl.gov`) whose first data-partner window closed in August 2026 and initial deliverables (technical report, weights, demonstrations) targeted for later in 2026; the **GS1 model architecture** as a trillion-parameter-class descendant of the **Trinity** sparse-MoE lineage documented in the *Arcee Trinity Large Technical Report* (~400B total / ~13B active parameters, fine-grained routed experts plus an always-active shared expert, sigmoid routing with soft-clamped momentum expert-bias updates, interleaved local/global attention with RoPE and positional-encoding-free layers, GQA with QK-normalization and gated attention, depth-scaled sandwich RMSNorm, a 200K byte-level BPE tokenizer, **512K-token context**, 17T training tokens, Muon-family optimizer and FP8 serving); the **merging, distillation and targeted-training toolchain** of **MergeKit** (LGPL-3.0; linear, SLERP, TIES, DARE-TIES, task arithmetic, model stock, CMA-ES evolutionary merging, LoRA extraction, MoE upcycling, out-of-core loading on CPU or 8 GB VRAM), **DistillKit** (Apache-2.0 online/offline logit and hidden-state distillation with composable KL/JSD/TVD and ranking losses and ~300 bytes/token logit compression) and **Spectrum** (random-matrix-theory SNR-targeted layer freezing, up to 42% faster training and 36% lower GPU memory); the **open-weight portfolio** of Apache-2.0 **AFM-4.5B** (dense decoder-only, GQA, ReLU², 64K context, 8T tokens across eleven languages, DatologyAI curation, SFT + RLHF with verifiable reward models), **Trinity-Mini** (26B total / ~3B active, 128 experts, 128K context, 10T tokens), **Caller** (32B tool calling), **Homunculus** (12B `/think` and `/nothink` modes) and the **Virtuoso**, **SuperNova-Medius** and **Arcee-Blitz** distillations; and the **governed deployment stack** of Arcee Conductor routing, Arcee Orchestra agentic workflows on the AWS Marketplace and Arcee Foundry with SaaS, private-VPC, on-premises and **air-gapped** modes. A *Mission Domains* sub-bullet retains the HPC, materials, energy, fusion, climate and high-energy-physics workbench scope.

- **Abstract & Executive Summary Redraft**: Restructured the abstract into multi-paragraph narrative blocks with explicit sub-bulleted breakdowns for Executive Order 14363, Federal Register 90 FR 55035 codification, Title 3 CFR legal mandates, core platforms (**AmSC**, **HPDF**, **OPAL**, **ModCon**, **SPOTTER-AI**, **Genesis Open Models** `genesisopenmodels.anl.gov`), and strategic domains.
- **Section 1 (Introduction & Context) Restructuring**:
  - Transformed introduction into narrative paragraphs highlighting federal executive orders and $5B+ federal alignment.
  - Sub-bulleted 11 major federal agency portfolios under §1.1 (**OSTP**, **DOE & NNSA**, **DOC NIST**, **NSF**, **NIH/HHS**, **NASA**, **Department of War/DOD**, **DHS S&T**, **DOI/USGS**, **USDA/AgARDA**, **AAU**).
  - Expanded Section 1.2 into an explicit 4-Tier Architectural Topology (Tier 1 Executive Governance, Tier 2 Dual Foundries & HPC Grid, Tier 3 Interagency Orchestration, Tier 4 153 Core Execution Nodes).
  - Formatted core mission objectives 1–5 in §1.3 into clean numbered lists.
- **Section 2 (Technical Framework & Core Pillars) Restructuring**:
  - **§2.1 (HPC & AI Grid)**: Structured into sub-bulleted categories for Exascale supercomputers (*Frontier*, *Aurora*, *El Capitan*), dedicated AI nodes (*Lux*, *Discovery*, *Solstice*, *Equinox*, *Doudna*, *Cech*, *Crossroads*, *Mission*, *Vision*), AI accelerators (SambaNova SN40L, Cerebras WSE-3, Groq LPUs, Dell AI Factory), $1B U.S.-Japan Bilateral Partnership, and FAIR highways & microelectronics EDA ($83M NSF Highways, Synopsys.ai 50x acceleration, Micron HBM3e/4, Cornelis OPX / Nokia Bell PQC).
  - **§2.2 (Quantum & CHIPS Act)**: Formatted Executive Order mandates, DOE Quantum Genesis Initiative ($2B, 150–250 logical qubits by 2028), National Quantum User Facility, QC-ADDS program, and $2.013B CHIPS Act LOIs into clean numbered priorities. Structured all 10 quantum companies/foundries (IBM Quantum Anderon 300mm, GlobalFoundries QTS, Quantinuum Helios, Atom Computing, Diraq, PsiQuantum, Infleqtion, Rigetti, D-Wave, xLight EUV) into dedicated sub-bulleted entries.
  - **§2.3 (Domain Applications & Workflows)**: Formatted domain categories A–D into sub-bulleted blocks covering High Energy Physics (CERN ATLAS, JLab, SLAC, BNL, Fermilab), Fusion Energy (AI4Fusion, ORNL/Cleveland Clinic/IBM FLiBe quantum simulation), Autonomous Self-Driving Cloud Labs (NVIDIA Omniverse, Google Gemini, Microsoft Discovery, Everstar, UTA AURORA Cloud Lab, JHU APL, Emerald Cloud Lab), and Nuclear/Grid Security (INL Project Prometheus, NETL, NREL/Atom Computing ARIES, Ames AIM-MAG $\text{Fe}_{16}\text{N}_2$, MIRAGE SciDAC, SRNL VITA-SCALE, SHINE, Albemarle/Ramaco).
  - **§2.4 (Flagship Projects)**: Organized projects under Sub-sections A–E into structured sub-bullets capturing *Consortium Scope*, *Technical Innovation*, and *Impact Metric*.

Synthesized flagship Genesis Mission research awards, interagency grants (DOE DE-FOA-0003612, NSF 26-023, NIH $1.2B Bio Genesis pool, CHIPS Act microelectronics awards), and domain breakthroughs across 5 primary scientific domains:
  1. *Advanced Nuclear Energy, Autonomous Reactor Operations & Regulatory Compliance* (Project Prometheus, Gordian AI, SHINE AMUSE/ARTEMIS PINN fuel recycling).
  2. *Fusion Plasma Physics & Quantum-Centric Reactor Material Discovery* (AI4Fusion disruption control, ORNL-IBM Quantum FLiBe molten salt calculations).
  3. *Autonomous Self-Driving Laboratories, Physical AI & Edge Resilience* (Utah AURORA Cloud Lab, UT Arlington sub-millisecond EPICS trust layer, closed-loop synthesis pipelines).
  4. *Particle Acceleration, High Energy Physics & Quantum Infrastructure* (ATLAS GNN trigger grid, NREL neutral-atom Quantum-in-the-Loop power grid, UNL 6G RAN autopilot co-designer, xLight EUV FEL lithography source).
  5. *Environmental Remediation, Critical Minerals & Agri-Genomics* (SRNL VITA-SCALE nuclear waste vitrification AI, Ames AIM-MAG rare-earth-free magnets, USDA germplasm AI, Arcee AI GS1 open-weight model family).

## [2.0.0] - 2026-08-10

### Major Release 2.0.0 — Complete Reference Citation Processing, Architectural Topology & Agentic Science Benchmark

The **v2.0.0** release marks a major architectural milestone for the **Genesis Mission** documentation repository. This release consolidates complete reference index processing (354 / 354 external citation sources processed), regenerates the core system architecture and strategic flow topology (§1.2), expands the strategic mission objectives into 5 comprehensive pillars (§1.3), and enforces 100% bimodal language parity across English (`README.md`) and German (`README.de.md`) editions.

### Key Highlights & Major Accomplishments

- **100% Master Reference Citation Processing (354 / 354 Entries Processed)**:
  - **Complete Audit & Enrichment**: Fully digested all 354 indexed external reference citations across 7 thematic sections (White House releases, DOE FOA DE-FOA-0003612 solicitations, NIST CHIPS Act LOIs, 17 DOE National Lab disclosures, 5 NNSA sites, 61 industry partners, and 57 research universities).
  - **Reference Index Verification**: Achieved 100% canonicalization, validation, deduplication, and zero-unprocessed-entry state tracking in `reference_coverage.md`.

- **Regenerated Architectural Topology & System Flow (§1.2)**:
  - **Four-Tiered System Architecture**: Updated the core system topology diagram to incorporate interagency governance (White House OSTP, DOE, DOC, NSF, NIH/HHS, DOD, DHS S&T, NASA, DOI, USDA), twin foundries (Quantum Leadership & Microelectronics Foundries + AI for Science & High-Performance Computing Grid), the federated interagency orchestration layer (American Science Cloud & Security Platform AmSC, Transformational AI Models Consortium ModCon, Genesis Open Models registry `genesisopenmodels.anl.gov`, SPOTTER-AI provenance threat-tracing engine, $83M NSF FAIR data highways), and 148 public-private-academic execution nodes.
  - **Diagram Syntax & Structural Parity**: Repaired ASCII art line corruption in `README.de.md` and established complete German language parity.

- **Expanded Strategic Mission Objectives (§1.3)**:
  - **5 Comprehensive Strategic Pillars**:
    1. *Convergent Heterogeneous Compute Substrate & Federated Grid*: Federation of exascale supercomputers (*Frontier* #1, *Aurora*, *El Capitan* #1), high-density AI nodes (*Lux* MI355X, *Solstice*, *Equinox*, *Doudna*, *Crossroads*, *Mission*, *Vision*), reconfigurable dataflow units (SambaNova SN40L), wafer-scale systems (Cerebras WSE-3 CS-3), high-bandwidth memory (Micron HBM3e/HBM4), and 7 quantum hardware modalities.
    2. *Closed-Loop Agentic Scientific Discovery & Self-Driving Automation*: Autonomous multi-agent AI networks (Google Gemini/AI Co-Scientist/AlphaFold 3/AlphaEarth, Microsoft Discovery/MatterGen/MatterSim, Anthropic Claude 3.7 Sonnet/MCP, OpenAI FedRAMP enclaves, Meta SAM 3 & DINOv3, FutureHouse PaperQA/ChemCrow, LILA) orchestrating self-driving cloud laboratories (University of Utah Price Engineering **AURORA Cloud Lab**, Emerald Cloud Lab, JHU APL, Cleveland Clinic FLiBe pipeline).
    3. *Domestic Microelectronics, Quantum & Advanced Manufacturing Sovereignty*: Re-shoring advanced semiconductor manufacturing, onshore 300mm quantum qubit foundries (IBM Anderon in Albany NY, GlobalFoundries Quantum Technology Solutions), and extreme ultraviolet lithography (xLight $150M free-electron laser EUV prototype at Albany NanoTech with NIST & Fermilab SRF cryomodules) under $2.013B CHIPS Act LOIs and $2B DOE Quantum Genesis Initiative.
    4. *National Security, Defense Biosecurity & Geopolitical Leadership*: Department of War ($200M FY26 / $1.3B+ FY27) DB-FORGE biosecurity, NNSA Secret/Restricted Data Enterprise Cloud with AWS (LANL *Crossroads*, *Mission*, *Vision*), NNSA *Aires Tide* AI flight vehicle (7x faster, 15x cheaper), DHS S&T critical infrastructure software verification and bio-threat attribution, and ANL SPOTTER-AI scientific provenance threat tracing.
    5. *Energy Independence, Biomedical Breakthroughs & Critical Material Resilience*: Clean energy grid stabilization (NREL ARIES + Atom Computing co-simulation, Qubit Engineering Neuro-Grid, TVA MOU), Small Modular Reactor licensing (INL Project Prometheus 50% licensing/cost reduction, NRAD remote control, Clinch River SMR), tokamak fusion plasma control (PPPL AI4Fusion, UW-Madison real-time control, FLiBe tritium breeding), environmental cleanup (SRNL VITA-SCALE vitrification saving $150B+), biomedical discovery (NIH/HHS $1.2B Bio Genesis Mission across 6 National S&T Challenges cutting discovery timelines by 50%), and critical mineral independence (Ames AIM-MAG, Albemarle DLE lithium, Niron Clean Earth Magnets $Fe_{16}N_2$, Ramaco coal-to-graphite, Accenture CM2US).

- **Full Bimodal Language & Repository Synchronization**:
  - Complete structural, technical, numerical, and citation parity between `README.md` and `README.de.md`.
  - Incremented major version tag to **`2.0.0`** across all primary documentation targets.

## [1.0.0] - 2026-08-08

### Initial Major Release — The Genesis Mission Reference Architecture

The **v1.0.0** release establishes the definitive, open-source technical reference paper and ecosystem topology index for the **Genesis Mission**: a multi-billion-dollar federal initiative coupling Artificial Intelligence (AI), fault-tolerant quantum computing, and exascale high-performance computing (HPC).

### Key Highlights
- **100% Ecosystem Coverage (148 Core Flagship Nodes)**:
  - **17 DOE National Laboratories**: Complete technical profile for ANL, BNL, INL, LBNL, LLNL, LANL, NETL, NREL, ORNL, PNNL, PPPL, SNL, SRNL, SLAC, TJNAF, Fermilab, and Ames.
  - **61 Industry & Hyperscale Partners**: Architectural integration covering AWS, Google DeepMind, Microsoft, NVIDIA, AMD, Dell Technologies, HPE, IBM Quantum, SambaNova Systems, Cerebras, Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta AI, Scale AI, Hugging Face, FutureHouse, LILA, Applied Materials, Synopsys, Siemens, xLight, and key critical materials/energy partners.
  - **9 Federal Agencies & Executive Bodies**: Governance overview spanning White House OSTP, DOE Office of Science, DOC NIST/CHIPS R&D Office, NSF, NIH/HHS, NASA, DOD (Department of War), DHS S&T, and DOI.
  - **4 Specialized Research & Healthcare Hubs**: Profiles for Cleveland Clinic, Johns Hopkins University APL, AI Tennessee Initiative, and RTI International.
  - **57 Awardee Research Universities**: Categorized academic nodes across quantum science, AI/SciML, materials discovery, nuclear physics, and grid decarbonization.
- **Three Core Technical Pillars**:
  1. *Heterogeneous HPC Substrate*: Exascale supercomputers (*Frontier*, *Aurora*, *El Capitan*, *Solstice*, *Equinox*, *Lux*, *Discovery*), Dell AI Factory, and SambaNova Reconfigurable Dataflow RDUs.
  2. *Quantum Leadership & CHIPS Act Foundries*: Over $2B in DOE quantum leadership programs matched by $2B+ Commerce CHIPS Act LOIs spanning 7 modalities (superconducting, trapped-ion, neutral-atom, photonic, silicon spin, and cross-modality foundries).
  3. *Closed-Loop Agentic Scientific Workflows*: Autonomous AI agents orchestrating quantum-classical algorithms, robotic wet labs, and real-time sensor streams (synchrotrons, tokamaks, accelerators).
- **Strategic Capital Mechanics**: Detailed allocation architecture tracking >$3.5 Billion across FOA grants, national security challenges, CHIPS Act incentives, and hyperscaler compute credits.
- **Curated Reference Index**: Comprehensive external citation index in `references.md` mapping federal releases, FOA solicitations, lab disclosures, and corporate announcements.
